Preserve string formatting when already a string.\n    if val is None:\n        return \"\"\n    if isinstance(val, str):\n        return val\n    if isinstance(val, bool):\n        return \"true\" if val else \"false\"\n    return str(val)\n\n\ndef flatten_json(obj, prefix=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Flatten nested JSON into lookup keys.\n\n    - Dict keys join with '.'\n    - List indexes use '[i]'\n\n    Examples:\n      {\"a\": {\"b\": 1}} -> {\"a.b\": \"1\"}\n      {\"items\": [{\"name\": \"x\"}]} -> {\"items[0].name\": \"x\"}\n\n    Also adds the bare prefix key if obj is a scalar.\n    \"\"\"\n    out = {}\n\n    def rec(x, pfx):\n        if isinstance(x, dict):\n            for k, v in x.items():\n                k = str(k)\n                np = f\"{pfx}.{k}\" if pfx else k\n                rec(v, np)\n        elif isinstance(x, list):\n            for i, v in enumerate(x):\n                np = f\"{pfx}[{i}]\" if pfx else f\"[{i}]\"\n                rec(v, np)\n        else:\n            if pfx:\n                out[pfx] = stringify(x)\n\n    rec(obj, prefix)\n    return out\n\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{\\s*(.+?)\\s*\\}\\}\")\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(text, lookup):\n    if text is None or \"{{\" not in text:\n        return text, False\n\n    changed = False\n\n    def replacer(match):\n        nonlocal changed\n        key = match.group(1).strip()\n        if key in lookup:\n            changed = True\n            return stringify(lookup[key])\n        return match.group(0)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(replacer, text)\n    return new_text, (new_text != text)\n\n\ndef localname(tag):\n    # 2) Handle placeholders split across multiple <hp:t> inside a single <hp:run>\n    #    Strategy: concatenate run texts, replace, then store result in first <hp:t>,\n    #    blanking subsequent <hp:t> while preserving elements.\n    run_tag = f\"{{{hp_ns}}}run\"\n    t_tag = f\"{{{hp_ns}}}t\"\n\n    for run in root.iter(run_tag):\n        t_elems = [c for c in run if c.tag == t_tag]\n        if len(t_elems) <= 1:\n            continue\n\n        combined = \"\".join([(te.text or \"\") for te in t_elems])\n        if \"{{\" not in combined:\n            continue\n\n        new_combined, changed = replace_placeholders_in_text(combined, lookup)\n        if not changed:\n            continue\n\n        t_elems[0].text = new_combined\n        for te in t_elems[1:]:\n            # Preserve structure but avoid duplicating leftover fragments\n            te.text = \"\"\n\n        p = find_parent_paragraph(run, hp_ns)\n        if p is not None:\n            modified_paragraphs.add(p)\n\n    #
